Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the navier-stokes structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 190. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 902 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to navier-stokes theory, we decompose the problem into 190 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 902. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
